# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 006 PACKAGE REGION EFFECTS REGISTRY
# ============================================================================
# Shared current-map metadata, weight, and reservation publishers for the
# regional Event 006 package pool.
# Regional source headings are condensed; package blocks and research notes
# remain grouped by their original region.
# ============================================================================

# REGION 01 SOURCE: 006_independence_wave_packages_region_01_effects.txt
# Exact current-map package publishers for region 01. Each loader establishes
# static registry metadata and scopes; the reservation effect then publishes
# the anchor first, compact states second, and extended territory last.
independence_wave_load_package_iw_001 = {
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_candidate_package_id = constant:independence_wave_package_id.iw_001 }
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_candidate_reservation_group = constant:independence_wave_reservation_group_id.rg_121_120_133 }
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_candidate_region = constant:independence_wave_region.northern_western_europe }
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_candidate_depth = constant:independence_wave_package_depth.regional }
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_candidate_archetype = constant:independence_wave_package_archetype.port_or_island }
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_candidate_disposition = constant:independence_wave_pool_disposition.automatic_if_not_living }
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_candidate_uses_registered_tag = constant:independence_wave_planner.selected }
	every_possible_country = {
		limit = { tag = SCO }
		save_event_target_as = liberation_candidate_country
	}
	121 = {
		save_event_target_as = liberation_candidate_anchor
		owner = { save_event_target_as = liberation_candidate_primary_host }
	}
}

independence_wave_prepare_weight_iw_001 = {
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_001 = constant:independence_wave_allocation_factor.zero }
	if = { limit = { can_plan_independence_wave_package_iw_001 = yes } independence_wave_load_package_iw_001 = yes independence_wave_calculate_candidate_allocation_weight = yes set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_001 = independence_wave_candidate_weight } add_to_temp_variable = { independence_wave_region_01_total_weight = independence_wave_candidate_weight } }
}

independence_wave_prepare_weight_iw_093 = {
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_093 = constant:independence_wave_allocation_factor.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			can_plan_independence_wave_package_iw_093 = yes
			is_independence_wave_high_chaos_pool_open = yes
		}
		independence_wave_load_package_iw_093 = yes
		independence_wave_calculate_candidate_allocation_weight = yes
		set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_093 = independence_wave_candidate_weight }
		add_to_temp_variable = { independence_wave_region_09_total_weight = independence_wave_candidate_weight }
	}
}

# published twice.

# REGION 11 SOURCE: 006_independence_wave_packages_region_11_effects.txt
# Exact current-map package publishers for bound region 11 packages. Loaders
# establish registry metadata and scopes; reservation effects publish each
# fixed anchor through the shared reservation lifecycle. The accepted compact
# field duplicates the anchor for every package in this slice, so no state is
# published twice.

independence_wave_prepare_weight_iw_134 = {
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_134 = constant:independence_wave_allocation_factor.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { can_plan_independence_wave_package_iw_134 = yes }
		independence_wave_load_package_iw_134 = yes
		independence_wave_calculate_candidate_allocation_weight = yes
		set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_134 = independence_wave_candidate_weight }
		add_to_temp_variable = { independence_wave_region_12_total_weight = independence_wave_candidate_weight }
	}
}

independence_wave_prepare_weight_iw_150 = {
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_150 = constant:independence_wave_allocation_factor.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { can_plan_independence_wave_package_iw_150 = yes is_independence_wave_high_chaos_pool_open = yes }
		independence_wave_load_package_iw_150 = yes
		independence_wave_calculate_candidate_allocation_weight = yes
		set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_150 = independence_wave_candidate_weight }
		add_to_temp_variable = { independence_wave_region_13_total_weight = independence_wave_candidate_weight }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Per-package automatic weight preparation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

independence_wave_prepare_weight_iw_180 = {
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_180 = constant:independence_wave_allocation_factor.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { can_plan_independence_wave_package_iw_180 = yes }
		independence_wave_load_package_iw_180 = yes
		independence_wave_calculate_candidate_allocation_weight = yes
		set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_180 = independence_wave_candidate_weight }
		add_to_temp_variable = { independence_wave_region_14_total_weight = independence_wave_candidate_weight }
	}
}

independence_wave_prepare_weight_iw_182 = {
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_182 = constant:independence_wave_allocation_factor.zero }
	# GZX is a dormant shell until the full identity, portrait, and gameplay
	# attestation is promoted. Mirror the execution-id gate used by the admitted
	# California carrier so an unadmitted candidate cannot enter the frozen
	# allocation and fail only at release preflight.
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_execution_package_id = constant:independence_wave_package_id.iw_182 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			can_plan_independence_wave_package_iw_182 = yes
			has_independence_wave_runtime_package_content_attestation_for_execution_id = yes
		}
		independence_wave_load_package_iw_182 = yes
		independence_wave_calculate_candidate_allocation_weight = yes
		set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_182 = independence_wave_candidate_weight }
		add_to_temp_variable = { independence_wave_region_14_total_weight = independence_wave_candidate_weight }
	}
}

	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_weight_iw_184 = constant:independence_wave_allocation_factor.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { independence_wave_execution_package_id = constant:independence_wave_package_id.iw_184 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			can_plan_independence_wave_package_iw_184 = yes
			has_independence_wave_runtime_package_content_attestation_for_execution_id = yes

	863 = { independence_wave_try_candidate_compact_state = yes }
